# boot/mentl.wasm — the pinned fixpoint compiler

This binary IS Mentl: the wheel compiled by itself to the byte-identical
fixed point (first light, 2026-07-10, commit 87c0152, tag `first-light`).
It replaces the hand-WAT seed as the bootstrap: the default build loop is

    boot/mentl.wasm  compiles  src/**+lib/**  →  m2   (wheel-emitted)
    m2               compiles  src/**+lib/**  →  m3
    assert m2 == m3                                    (the fixpoint ratchet)

Because boot is itself wheel-emitted, m2 == m3 IS the fixed point — the
seed-era rule "NOT m2 == m3" (PLAN §6) compared a SEED-emitted m2 against a
wheel-emitted m3 and retired with the seed.

HOW AN ENTRY IS WRITTEN (2026-07-30 — the ritual's mechanical half
absorbed): `MARCH_REPIN=1 bash tools/march.sh` writes the block itself at
the moment it repins — the sha256 read from the artifact it just copied,
the verdict it just arbitrated, the line count and census it just
measured. Nobody retypes a measurement (the fabrication class, caught
live twice). The AUTHOR writes exactly one thing: the narrative line,
replacing the march's `‹NARRATIVE UNWRITTEN›` placeholder — and until
that is written, `tools/doc-truth.sh` REFUSES at every verify, so an
unblessed pin is mechanically visible instead of remembered.

Provenance, self-confirmed at pin time:

- source: THE CHECK CHASES, SO THE BUILD CAN SHARE
  (`Hβ.infer.instantiate-shares-never-clones`). subst_changes answered TRUE
  for every bound var — "an NBound var inlines its binding (always differs)"
  — and in a JUDGED decl most vars are bound, so check-then-build was
  present and never got to answer no: every polymorphic reference rebuilt
  the callee's whole type tree. The check now follows the edge and asks
  whether the CONTENT changes, so a bound cell holding no mapped var answers
  false and its entire subtree SHARES; only a path reaching a quantified var
  rebuilds, exactly as before. Privacy is untouched — the mapping decides
  first, so a quantified var still freshens per use. The opposite error
  (answering by the mapping alone) was built and measured on the way here:
  a quantified var behind a binding goes unseen and polymorphism severs
  silently. This is one face of rung 3 landing alone; the publication half
  is blocked on the read path, and the §7 ledger entry carries what three
  configurations measured about that wall.

- source: THE WARM PATH WAS HALF-KEYED, AND THE GATE THAT KNEW IT STOPPED
  ONE LINE SHORT. The previous pin (f58dfc10) made a module's identity its
  resolved path — and moved the tree scan and manifest onto that key while
  split_weave_by_module still bucketed the analyzed statements by NAME. So
  assoc_stmts answered EMPTY for every cached module and the incremental
  compile emitted the cone alone: 2933 bytes against a cold compile's 37644,
  the whole restored program dropped. The frontier's warm-inc leg has a
  divergence check that catches exactly this, and it never ran, because the
  cone-line check above it failed on a RENDERING change (the cone prints
  paths now) and returned. One key at the last site fixes the bug; the leg no
  longer returns on an independent failure, which is the half that matters,
  because a leg that halts at its first red hides the rest of its own
  coverage. Also here: the census leg keeps its children's stderr and exit
  codes, so a query that DIES is no longer reported as a shape that is
  missing — a diagnostic whose name can lie, in the gate built to catch
  those. frontier 374/0 (warm-inc incremental == cold, byte-identical),
  verify green, march CLEAN m2 == m3. The §7 ledger entry of the same name
  carries the arc, including what the prior entry claimed and why it was
  true-as-measured and wrong.

  (CLEAN m2 == m3; re-pinned from m2 per march.sh — 418862 lines, census 0)

- source: A MODULE'S IDENTITY WAS THE SPELLING THAT REACHED IT. The DAG
  walk keyed its visited set by the module NAME and resolved to a path on
  the next line, so `lists` and `lib/lists` — one file — collected the same
  source twice and every declaration in it collided with itself: 58
  diagnostics on a four-line program whose only content was one import. An
  import is an edge, and drawing an edge that already exists is a no-op.
  Identity is the resolved path now at all five sites that compared it
  (visited set, dep edges, partition wait condition, tree-scan closure,
  entry filter) — half-keying it is worse than the bug, because a
  name-matched dep edge against a path-keyed DAG is dropped and the
  partition then runs an importer before its dep. Three re-derivations
  deleted with it: driver_check_module's second path resolution and second
  file read, and rederive_cone's resolution of a name already a path. No
  gate had ever seen this because the wheel's own build is the cat-blob
  through stdin, which resolves no imports, and every lib spells its
  siblings bare — tripwire 3 whole. Gate born RED at 58, green at 0
  (tests/syntax/import-path-spelling.mn). Landing also carries
  tools/thread-gate.sh (the compile's concurrency width, ratcheted, seen RED
  first) and a wt-env.sh probe fix whose defect could fire only on the
  pinned LTS. The §7 ledger entry of the same name carries the arc.
- boot/mentl.wasm  sha256 f58dfc1070f5c7a56edaf44b7546980ebf6def3a6ab0c5fba19f9e51e9c8797d
  (CLEAN m2 == m3; re-pinned from m2 per march.sh — 418856 lines, census 0)

- source: THREE DEAD FNS THE FACET FOUND, AND A CONFIRMATION THAT ITS
  OVER-REPORT IS WHAT IT SAYS IT IS. Reading the orphan-claims rows for
  backends/wasm: ty_to_wasm_type (callers reach repr_wat directly, 19
  references, so the repr_of |> repr_wat composition had no consumer),
  handle_recorded (its one reference is its own recursion — the facet
  counts refs OUTSIDE the declaration, which is exactly the distinction
  that makes a self-recursive orphan visible), and emit_load_chain (a
  one-line wrapper defaulting leaf_repr to RI32, while every caller uses
  emit_load_chain_repr — 4 references). All three deleted; the comment
  moved onto the surviving fn. THE EMIT LINE COUNT DID NOT CHANGE, which
  is the independent confirmation: reachability had already pruned them,
  so this is source hygiene rather than a behaviour change. The other
  backends/wasm rows are TYPE names, and cursor_transport's three —
  Surface, Action, PatchWrite — are EFFECT names carried in cursor_step's
  row, where type position draws no reference edge. That is the
  over-report the facet's own entry names, measured rather than assumed.

- source: THE LARGEST CLAIM IN THE WHEEL WAS INERT. The orphan-claims facet
  named `ic_fixpoint_handler` on its first run — 3,245 bytes of prose, zero
  references — and the question it left was whether that was built-ahead or
  abandoned. Reading the arm answered neither: BOTH branches called
  `cursor_argmax_compute(caret)` identically, so the `last_epoch` state
  changed no result and the memoization its prose described did not exist.
  Its comment made two false claims in one block — "installed via `~>`
  above" (installed nowhere) and "STOPS re-projecting" (it re-projected
  either way). Deleted: it removed no capability, and leaving it was the
  real risk, because anything installing it on the strength of the prose
  would have got zero caching and believed IC was live. The design fault it
  hid is kept at the site and in RESIDUE: an epoch alone cannot key this
  read, because cursor_argmax takes a CARET and moving the caret without
  editing leaves the epoch unchanged while the right cursor differs. The
  key is (epoch, caret) — `Hβ.cursor.cached-argmax-keyed-by-epoch-and-
  caret`, with the fixture that would have caught it named.

- source: THE PATTERN BEHIND THREE FINDS BECAME A VERB. BodyContext, Delta
  and the drifted verify_candidate copy were all the same shape: a
  declaration nothing references, carrying a paragraph explaining how it is
  used. Someone wrote down a belief the graph disagrees with, and each one
  cost a hand read to notice. `mentl query <file> "orphan-claims"` reports
  the structural conjunction — zero outside references AND 240+ bytes of
  attached prose, read live through graph_comment_at. It does NOT read
  English: deciding that "read by LEvPerform" is a claim would need to parse
  meaning, and a checker that guesses would be a second, worse comment-ref
  tier. The existing comment-ref ratchet covers the name layer (a BACKTICKED
  name resolving nowhere) and stayed at zero through all three finds,
  because every phantom reader was named without backticks. First run: 216
  declarations, and the largest is `cursor -> ic_fixpoint_handler` at 3,245
  bytes with zero references — a handler nothing installs at all, one step
  past Delta, banked as `Hβ.cursor.ic-fixpoint-handler-is-never-installed`
  rather than deleted, because unlike Delta its shipping alternative is not
  established.

- source: THE GRADIENT'S INVERSE DIRECTION HAD TWO IMPLEMENTATIONS AND THE
  SHIPPING ONE IS THE DIAGNOSTIC. gradient_delta.mn is deleted whole — the
  Delta effect, delta_default, four arms and nineteen supporting fns, 407
  lines. delta_pick had zero references; its three siblings had exactly one
  each, all inside delta_pick's own arm, so the four ops formed a closed
  ring nothing entered, while delta_default was installed at three sites in
  main.mn. Its comment named a reader — the Cursor handler's
  CursorView.teach — that does not exist in cursor.mn. What settles it as a
  deletion rather than a wiring job is main.mn:633, which already says
  "T_OverDeclared already IS a proposal — the diagnostic": `mentl tighten`
  reads the warnings the judgment banked, and authored 174 of them at pin
  1aca4868, while Delta walked the graph per handle to RE-DERIVE the same
  verdict. Carried-Truth at the exact shape the law names, and the fix is
  toward less code. Wheel 59,296 → 58,983 lines; emit 420,007 → 417,799;
  the performs roster 78 effects → 77.

- source: THE PROOF GATE HAD TWO HOMES, AND THE DEAD ONE HAD DRIFTED. The
  performs facet reported `mentl -> Synth 1/3`, so the two unperformed ops
  got read at their sites — and verify_candidate's arm turned out to hold a
  second copy of what "proven" means. The fan path's own comment said so:
  "the nested trio mirrors the sequential verify_candidate arm exactly." It
  did not mirror it. candidate_judge instantiates the target FRESH so the
  proof never binds the hole's live frontier; the arm passed the live target
  straight into `verify_after_apply(h) && refinement_admits(target_ty, h)`.
  The copy nothing called was also the wrong copy, which is what a second
  home eventually becomes — and this is §1's own gate, the one that makes
  "any intelligence may propose, nothing executes unproven" mean something.
  candidate_proven is the single definition now; both the fan and the arm
  call it. The facet still reports 1/3 after the fix, which is correct: the
  duplication is gone, the op still has no performers, and that is the
  honest state of a contract op an external Synth handler would implement.
  Also banked: the peak ratchet falsely refuses every first march after a
  source edit, measured three landings running at ~100MB inflation
  (2,310,816 / 2,316,080 / 2,311,408 first, ~2,210,000 settled each time).

- source: THE HAND CLASSIFICATION BECAME A VERB. Three landings running,
  the find was the same shape — an effect whose ops nothing performs —
  and each time it cost a hand read: BodyContext (four ops, zero readers,
  installed in every lowering route), the Filesystem bypass, Interact (22
  ops, 14 unperformed). The `unreferenced` facet could surface those names
  but not name the shape, because its rows are bare names and telling an op
  from a fn from a type meant reading each one. `mentl query <file>
  "performs"` groups by the effect that declares them and answers k/n per
  roster. It reports 78 effects, 23 with ops nothing performs, and its
  independent read of Interact says 8/22 — the same split measured by hand
  one pin earlier, which is the check that the verb agrees with the method
  it replaces. A handler ARM is deliberately not a perform: an arm is the
  promise's other half, never its consumer, so k/n reads as how much of a
  surface is actually reached. The compiler's own effects sit mostly at
  n−1 (GraphRead 20/21, GraphWrite 28/29, InferCtx 16/17), which is the
  dormant-vocabulary class already carrying named peers; the wide gaps are
  Interact at 8/22 and the library rosters no wheel code performs.

- source: THE SUBSTRATE QUESTION GOT MEASURED, AND THE ANSWER UNBLOCKED THE
  FILE SERVER. Seven bypass sites sat behind one unknown: does a handler's
  extent survive a self-tail-calling loop? `mentl space` needs it, because
  its accept loop never returns, and guessing inside a sweep is how a wrong
  answer gets canonized. tests/micros/mn-handler-extent-tail-loop.mn asks
  directly — install outside, perform inside every iteration — and answers
  10: the extent survives AND the handler's state accumulates across
  iterations (1+2+3+4), which is the half a server depends on. So space_run
  installs wasi_filesystem over its loop and the file server performs
  fs_exists / fs_read_file. The install immediately made the medium REFUSE
  space_conn and space_loop for declaring `WASI` while performing
  Filesystem — that refusal is the proof the bypass had suppressed, and both
  signatures are honest now. driver_warm_persist's mkdir converted too (its
  row had declared Filesystem all along). persist_write was converted and
  REVERTED by the frontier's persist shadow leg, which is the sharper
  finding: `fs_write_file` answers E_MissingVariable in that subset link
  because the Filesystem effect lives in src/types.mn and lib/persist.mn
  must compile standalone — a lib/ module cannot perform this effect, so the
  impl there is forced rather than lazy, and that is a floor under the
  ratchet. Seam 11 → 8. A peak-RSS breach of 816KB (0.035%) appeared on one
  march and was NOT raised: a second read of the same tree measured 104MB
  lower, the variance that ceiling's own comment documents.
- boot/mentl.wasm  sha256 055b396f7fadab8793ebe3937bf980a996b8af52364e73451015248a9936f0f7
  (CLEAN m2 == m3; re-pinned from m2 per march.sh — 418677 lines, census 0)

- source: THE LARGEST UNWIRED SURFACE IN THE WHEEL WAS A HIDDEN GAP. The
  `unreferenced` facet reported 51 names in voice.mn, and reading them found
  `Interact`: 22 ops, a handler implementing all of them, installed at three
  sites — and 14 ops that nothing has ever performed. The split is not
  random. The READING half is live (project_root, open_file, save_file,
  file_text, focus, caret at nine references, consult, propose); the WRITING
  and SESSION halves are not (tree_list, create_file, rename_path,
  delete_path, edit, speak, the four run ops, the four session ops). `edit`,
  the op the whole felt surface turns on, has zero performs — the Space
  session can open a file, read it, move the caret, consult and propose, and
  cannot apply a change. None of this was in RESIDUE.md, and PLAN's own law
  says a gap not in that file does not exist, so §11 Arc E's critical path
  had an unnamed hole in it. Named now as
  `Hβ.voice.interact-write-half-is-unwired`, with the op-by-op measurement.
  Also: the effect seam tightened 13 → 11 — mcp_run's two mkdirs moved
  inside its own `~> wasi_filesystem` bracket, where the op is reachable.
  The remaining 11 all stop at the same wall, recorded at the ratchet.

- source: ELEVEN CALLERS REACHED PAST THE FILESYSTEM EFFECT, AND THE ROW
  NEVER SAID SO. `wasi_filesystem`'s own comment promises that "a function
  that doesn't reach fs_* ops proves `with !Filesystem`" and that severance
  then drops path_open / fd_close from the binary. Three routes — march_run,
  main's emit path, mcp's gate — created, wrote, renamed, closed and
  unlinked files through the `_impl` fns instead, and every one of them was
  already inside a `~> wasi_filesystem` install, so the ops were reachable
  the whole time. The bypass was invisible because the impls charge WASI
  while the capability the handler names is Filesystem: the row went on
  saying those routes touched no filesystem. One line of main.mn had both
  spellings in one expression — `fs_exists_impl(gen_path)` beside
  `fs_read_file(gen_path)`. The effect was also missing an op for what two
  of those routes do most: `fs_create` (CREAT|TRUNC, write rights, returns
  the fd) is declared and armed now, which is the DEP that made reaching for
  the impl feel necessary. The gate is a new verify tier reading the
  medium's own refs answer per impl and counting sites outside the handler
  that owns them: MEASURED 24 before, 13 after. The 13 are named, not
  accepted — space's file server, battery_libs, persist, dsp/cfc, each
  wanting its own install-coverage reading.

- source: THE FACET'S FIRST HARVEST, AND IT CAUGHT A WRITE-ONLY HANDLER IN
  THE EMIT CHAIN OF EVERY ROUTE. `mentl query "unreferenced"` named eleven
  lowercase declarations in the compiler's own modules; reading each at its
  site — never deleting off the list — split them cleanly. BodyContext was
  the find: four ops, a handler, and an install in `emit_context`, whose
  two writers sit at backends/wasm.mn:2783-2784 and whose readers are
  nobody. Its own comment claimed "read by LEvPerform's offset arithmetic,"
  and `current_body_captures` measures zero references. That is the
  `resume_kinds` class PLAN §5.U names, alive in the emit chain of all
  seven lowering routes. Deleted whole, with `current_region`,
  `graph_reason_edge`, `graph_mint_at`, and `ls_current_lambda_handle` —
  the last dragging its `lambda_h` frame field, because deleting the op
  alone would have MADE a write-only ledger while curing one. Four more
  stayed: `graph_emitfn_at` and `graph_narrow_set` say in their own
  comments that they are dormant vocabulary under named peers,
  `diag_applicability` is the catalog projection's unread half, and
  `fs_close` is dead only because three callers reach past it to
  `fs_close_impl` — deleting that one would canonize the bypass. Wheel
  59378 → 59296 lines, emit 419046 → 418607.

- source: THE MEDIUM FINDS ITS OWN DEAD DECLARATIONS, AND FINDING THEM
  TOLD IT WHERE IT WAS DROPPING EDGES. `mentl query <file> "unreferenced"`
  answers, for every name the weave declares, how many reference sites lie
  outside its own declaration — the import read one altitude down and
  turned around. Nine dead fns came out of src/ by hand on 2026-09-04, one
  grep per name; this is one pass over the decls the weave already carries.
  Its first run reported 461 of 4,388, and reading that number is what
  found the real bug: a `match` on a constructor resolved it — arity and
  exhaustiveness are checked against the very scheme it finds — and then
  dropped the edge, so 46 names read as declared-and-never-reached while
  the graph had proved otherwise. `graph_ref_note` now fires at the pattern
  site for infer_var_ref's own reason, and the count fell 461 → 415. The
  remainder is mostly type NAMES, and that is a different finding with a
  different price: an annotation's type name is never resolved at all, so
  there is no edge to carry — banked as
  `Hβ.infer.type-name-in-annotation-never-resolves` with its repro.

- source: THE MEDIUM WRITES ITS OWN SOURCE, AND THE VERB CAN NO LONGER
  WRITE WHAT IT CANNOT READ BACK. `mentl tighten main` authored 174 of 223
  row narrowings across 27 files here, census 0 — the largest medium-authored
  diff the wheel has taken. It is only sound because the run before it was
  not: `mentl tighten src/cursor.mn` authored 132 changes and the march came
  back with 40 errors, because tighten wrote the row it INFERRED —
  `Cast(GNode)`, the type-parameterized instance — into a with-clause whose
  grammar takes value arguments alone. A self-authoring verb has exactly one
  soundness property, and this is it: author nothing you cannot re-parse.
  `row_is_authorable` had the guard for open tails and no guard at all for
  an effect argument carrying a type, so `every_eff_arg_authorable` is the
  fix and the whole of it. The anonymity ratchet rose one (384 → 385) under
  the medium's own 174 changes; that is the tier telling the truth about a
  tree the medium wrote, and it is raised in-commit with the measurement,
  not fixed.

- source: THE MEDIUM FINDS ITS OWN DEAD IMPORTS NOW. `mentl query <file>
  "imports"` answers every import edge in the weave with whether the imported
  module brings any name the importing one references — read off the refs
  column and the module cells, never off text. It reports 63 dead of 256 on
  the wheel's own source, where a shell census of the same question found 52:
  a grep cannot tell a used name from one written in a comment, which is
  exactly how three dead `import types` lines sat unseen long enough to put
  the compiler's whole op vocabulary in every user program. module_cells
  carries each cell's decls out with its path and span now — dropping them was
  what made every consumer re-derive a module's content from text.

- source: THREE DEAD IMPORTS PUT THE COMPILER IN EVERY USER PROGRAM.
  lib/lists, lib/strings and lib/threading each carried `import types` and
  referenced not one name from it — every apparent use was a comment mention.
  Those three lines placed ~170 compiler op names (Abort, FreshHandle,
  Consume, GraphRead/Write, Diagnostic, EnvRead/Write, Verify) in the
  namespace of any program that touches a list, so a user's `effect
  Backtrack { abort() }` silently lost to the compiler's. Deleting them takes
  `types` out of a bare weave: 7 modules to 6, prelude floor 6385 → 2737
  source lines. E_OpShadowsOp lands armed beside it, for the collisions that
  remain real — prelude genuinely declares `yield`, and a crown crucible had
  been declaring it too, compiling to 44 spurious errors inside lib and
  burying the one refusal it exists to prove. It reports 1 now.

  its offset landed on 0 by alphabetical luck, and it proves now instead.

- source: THE ROW VAR KEYS THE TWIN, SO THE OFFSET IS THE RECORD'S. SYNTAX's
  own worked example of row polymorphism was a measured silent wrong: a field
  read through an open-row parameter took its offset from the KNOWN set's
  ordering, so `u.name` returned a neighbour's value with no diagnostic
  anywhere. Two sites dropped the same handle — the specialization walk
  discarded the row var, so every record shape shared one body, and the
  substitution kept the row unresolved, so that body could not close its
  receiver. Keyed on the proven set and resolved through the pair, each shape
  mints its own twin and reads its own layout. Two banked expectations flipped
  and were re-derived by hand rather than re-banked: the list-pattern floor was
  a limitation canonized, not a contract.

- source: THE REGION INDEX'S BUCKET COUNT IS ITS OWN. It was the literal 65536
  in region_index_new and 65535 in both accessors' masks — one fact written
  three times — and `len` is `load_i32`, so the mask now reads the count from
  the list that already knows it. It landed ALONE, because the change it was
  the precondition for was refuted at the march.
  THE PROFILE THAT SENT IT: with the thread spawn gone, branch_bracket is
  STILL 55.95% inclusive — the thread was never the cost, the bracket is —
  and `list_filled_from` on Spans is 25.91% SELF, 24.04% of the whole run
  reached from branch_bracket alone. region_tracker's state initializer fills
  65536 slots one list_set at a time on EVERY install, and branch_bracket
  installs it per judged branch: ~28 million slot writes for `fn main() = 7`.
  THE KILL: making the count a handler CONFIG PARAM so a branch could size to
  its own statement TRAPPED m3 (exit 134, zero bytes), identically with a
  global and with a bare literal — so NOT the capture-read class
  branch_bracket's own comment names. Reverting the param alone marches
  CLEAN, isolating it to parameterising this handler at one of its three
  install sites. Unexplained; the next probe bisects them.
  A RATCHET CORRECTION: the peak ceiling cut to 2250000 last pin was set from
  ONE reading. Three pins of materially the same wheel read 2298592, 2174492
  and 2256804KB, so it was measuring noise and refused a clean fixpoint.
  2310000 now — a ceiling belongs above a spread, never on a point.

- source: THE SINGLETON JUDGE BLOCK STOPS SPAWNING. §11 5.2 serialized the
  parallel final at judge_window 1 on 2026-08-07; the window went to 1 and the
  SPAWN did not, so every layer branch still ran as a real task — one OS
  thread, created and joined, for no parallelism at all. A block of ONE now
  calls branch_judge directly (BranchRec = BrDirect | BrSpawned, decided by
  BLOCK SIZE so Phase 9.2's K=8 restores spawning with no edit here). The
  artifact authorised it: branch_bracket carries the private env overlay and
  the deferred diagnostics either way, graph/intern/ledgers/summaries "stay
  the root's live instances", and the `~> graph_handler` wrapper existed only
  to reproduce the sequential form that a spawned instance's empty world had
  broken.
  MEASURED: guest threads 433 -> 0 (peak 10, exactly the runner's own
  baseline); `fn main() = 7` 0.78s -> 0.58s; the frontier sweep 297.64s ->
  239.38s at 371/0; the m3 leg 2298592KB -> 2174492KB, its ceiling following
  down to 2250000.
  THE CROWN CAUGHT THE ONE REAL CONSEQUENCE: census rose to 2, both
  E_EffectMismatch on ImageAlloc, because a task's body row never reached its
  spawner and a direct call makes the branch's allocation visible.
  driver_check_module and rederive_cone now declare what they always
  performed — a row widened by DELETION, not by a new effect.

  for Hβ.driver.link-is-reachability named its last unenumerated risk as a
  measurement: which prelude names does the compiler MINT that source never
  writes? Read off the artifact — the quoted literals in lower, backends/wasm,
  parser, infer and pipeline, intersected with what lib publishes — the answer
  is 44 (lower/emit 28; parser and infer add delay, not, concat, last,
  drop_last, byte_at/byte_len, str_of_buf, str_payload and the float-render
  family). A measured LOWER BOUND, not a proven-complete set: names built by
  splicing a fold_sig are invisible to a literal scan, and that limit is
  stated rather than papered over.
  The enumeration found `str_literal_5` — an IDENTITY function (`fn
  str_literal_5(s) with Pure = s`) whose comment claimed it allocated a string
  from individual byte arguments and whose text named the bootstrap deleted on
  2026-07-10. It carried the four float sentinels AND a name-keyed entry in
  is_seq_op's chain plus a hand-written signature in infer's substrate table.
  Deleted whole: four call sites inlined to their literals, the fn gone, both
  table entries gone — a name-keyed special case retired, not renamed.
  The behaviour it guarded was PINNED FIRST at exit=15 and re-measured 15
  after; the new frontier leg encodes each sentinel as a bit, and its RED test
  returned exit=11, naming the NaN branch by arithmetic.

---

- source: COST IS A GRAPH READ, SO IT CAN BE RATCHETED. `mentl query <file>
  "cost"` reports modules linked, source lines processed and nodes minted —
  all read from the weave's own NModule cells and graph_next(), never a
  clock. A wall time is a host fact that varies per run and can only ever be
  reported; these three are identical every run, so the frontier now holds
  the PRELUDE FLOOR under a ceiling that may only fall: a bare `fn main() = 7`
  costs 7 modules and 6304 source lines, and a rise means the medium began
  processing more source to answer the same trivial question. Both halves
  seen RED (unknown-query on the prior boot; the real 6304 against an
  under-set ceiling).
  AND A RETRACTION. The previous pin claimed the compile is QUADRATIC in
  module count, fitted on four points spanning N=7..22. Its own named probe
  refuted it: eight entries spanning N=6..54 and 6.3k..58k DAG lines show
  cost-per-LINE moving only 1.7× against cost-per-MODULE's 2.0×, and the
  quadratic predicted `main` at 22.0s where it measures 12.05s. THE COMPILE
  IS LINEAR IN THE SOURCE IT PROCESSES. The new facet confirms it from an
  independent deterministic channel: 10.7 → 12.2 nodes minted per source
  line across that same 9× range.

- source: THE MEDIUM CAN SHOW ITS OWN DAG. `mentl query <file> "modules"`
  projects the weave's module set from the NModule cells the driver already
  mints per range — a graph read, never a second walk of the filesystem. It
  exists because this session needed the answer and had to hand-roll a shell
  transitive-closure loop over `grep '^import'` to get it: the driver PROVES
  the DAG on every invocation and could show it to nobody. Born RED against
  the prior pin (`error: unknown query: modules`); the frontier leg pins the
  count and two named members so it cannot pass on a bare number.
  WHAT THE HAND WALK MEASURED BEFORE THE VERB REPLACED IT: compile cost
  tracks the module COUNT, not the entry's size — 890-line driver.mn (20
  modules) costs 3.47s while 3594-line parser.mn (13 modules) costs 1.82s —
  and per-module cost RISES with the count (0.107s at 7 modules, 0.204s at
  22). The compile is quadratic in module count, which is §5.O's env class.

- source: ONE LEX PER COMPILE. infer_program_converged ran `src |> frontend`
  twice, and frontend is `lex |> parse_program`, so every compile lexed the
  whole concatenated program twice. The two PARSES are load-bearing — the
  final walks a fresh generation, which is what pstart reads — but a token is
  a value that mints nothing, so the second lex was pure re-derivation. Not an
  improvement to the condemned tower: its judgment, cadence and two
  generations are untouched, and rung 3 simply removes a caller.
  AND TWO KILLS, both from measurement. Deleting that lex moved the fixture
  0.00s, so lex is not the floor. A probe replacing import_edges' body with a
  literal moved it 0.78s to 0.50s, so the 0.28s discovery floor is TRAVERSING
  the module's tokens — and rewriting that traversal as a position recursion
  instead of a fold measured IDENTICAL, so it is the walk, not the carrier.
  The position form was reverted: its only justification was a speedup that
  did not exist, and the law prefers the fold. RESIDUE carries what ~11µs per
  token actually indicts.
- boot/mentl.wasm  sha256 d74ba9612f738c1c725e7e3430cf2971aa7589b459b93d9575aa727b99cee698

- source: THE DAG CARRIES WHAT THE WALK RESOLVED. The element was the module
  NAME alone, so every consumer re-derived the rest by name: the sort key
  re-resolved the path, the compile fold re-resolved and re-read it, the tree
  scan did both and re-lexed for imports, and driver_module_in_dag_deps did all
  three again. Path resolution is up to five fs_exists probes. The element is
  now (name, path, source, imports) with four projections; the missing case is
  None, not a "" sentinel; driver_module_in_dag_deps is deleted whole.
  MEASURED AND REPORTED AGAINST ITSELF: the probe that named this work removed
  the FIRST read+lex and saved 0.28s of a 0.74s floor, but this change removed
  the SECOND — warm in page cache — and the fixture went 0.74s to 0.78s, a 5%
  REGRESSION not isolated to a cause. Sweep wall is unchanged at 297.64s and
  all 368 legs pass. The form stays per CLAUDE.md ⟐; the cost is the double
  LEX, which carrying tokens deletes and this element is the substrate for.
  The quiet gate then refused all five `ref` markers this landing authored;
  dropping them left the emit byte-identical, so the inference had every
  borrow already and the annotations were noise the ratchet caught.

- source: THE DEP WALK STOPS PARSING. An import is a lexical fact, so the
  driver's module discovery reads it from the token stream (parser.mn's
  import_edges) instead of building a full AST per module and dropping it —
  a parse the judging pass then re-derived. The throwaway graph instance
  went with the parse it isolated; six dep-walk rows tightened to what the
  medium measured, two of them proving !Diagnostic. The PERF premise was
  REFUTED at the same pin: 0.74s against a 0.71s baseline, flat, so the
  prelude floor is not parse-dominated and decl-level demand belongs at the
  judge stage. LEDGER carries the kill.

- source: THE PIN CATCHES UP, AND FOUR EXPECTATIONS CATCH UP WITH IT — boot
  had drifted four CLEAN landings behind (the statement-span/dispatcher mint,
  the cursor declaration projection, the skip_ws_back dissolution, the
  feedback row join), so every gate in the frontier's boot suite was
  reporting green about a wheel that no longer matched source. Repinning made
  four legs red at once; all four derived to ONE cause, and none of them to
  the row join: the dispatcher mint starts a declaration's span at its `fn`
  KEYWORD where `parse_fn(pos + 1)` used to start it at the NAME. Measured
  side by side — boot `at 3:4-3:24`, fresh `at 3:1-3:24` — and the wider span
  is the truthful one, so the four banked values were the narrow pre-mint era
  canonized. Re-banked under §9.11 after deriving each by hand: the mcp
  refusal span, the own-unconsumed span, and two `Query:` prefixes that now
  carry the whole declaration instead of its bare name.

- source: THE DEMAND ANALYSIS LOSES ITS CONCAT SPINE — `spec_candidates_fix`
  accumulated onto `[d] ++ done`, a concat spine whose `list_index` is O(depth);
  both reads the fix makes of it (membership by mangled name, the per-base cap
  count) indexed it, so the two linear scans were quadratic in the accepted count.
  The buffer-counter (`make_list(64)` + count, `reach_grow`'s own shape) replaces
  the spine with O(1)-indexed flat slots. Seven dead worthiness-gate functions
  deleted with it. Measured: the concat-spine family (`spec_base_count` 7.95%,
  `spec_rec_name_seen` 5.41%) dominated the demand analysis; the buffer-counter
  dissolves the class.

- source: CONSTRUCTION IS REACHABILITY — the enumeration-reader
  relocation (`Hβ.lower.lowering-is-a-column`). The deleted name→edges
  index answered "what does NAME reference?" by walking trees that had
  all been constructed up front; the demand worklist inverts it — a name
  popping from the frontier CONSTRUCTS its decl, and the constructed
  body IS the edge list, read once. A dead decl never constructs at all,
  so emission and reachability agree by construction instead of by two
  passes kept in sync. Container-keep is reproduced at the name map
  (every arm name keys its whole container stmt), emission stays source
  order, and a no-main library seeds all decls. The wheel shrank; the
  LEDGER entry of the same name carries the arc.

- source: AN EFFECT INSTANCE HAS AN IDENTITY — a bare name at an
  instance-argument position folds at registration to the thing it
  names: an effect to its interned handle (EAEffect), a nullary ctor to
  its own (EACon). Both are constants, so row equality compares words
  and `Flow(1, Store)` is PROVABLY distinct from `Flow(1, Wasi)` — the
  crown's conservative EANode arm no longer swallows the ctor
  vocabulary the IFC lattice is built from. Four crown crucibles and the
  refinement-clause fixture, each seen RED against the prior boot (both
  sound cases over-refused, the contradiction double-reported).

- source: THE DELAY LINE IS AS DEEP AS IT SAYS — `delay(N)` carries N
  priors instead of the one every depth silently got. One read
  (`feedback_depth`) answers what the RHS declares, and every judgment
  about a line is an arm of it: the causality refusal, lower's slot
  count, the emit's declared globals and its shift. `E_ComputedDelayDepth`
  is the twelfth armed class — a depth no literal states cannot size a
  static line, and one slot is not an answer to a site that asked for n.

- source: THE CYCLE DISCIPLINE (2026-07-30; the §7 ledger entry of the
  same name — the schemes-are-edges arc's first rung). A Tarjan cycle
  judges under monomorphic recursion by default: at group entry an
  unsig'd member's env view re-registers with its TYPE cells shared

  param/ret cells instead of discarding its demand into a fresh copy —
  union-find propagation as the cycle's constraint channel. Row handles
  stay quantified (the full-mono probe measured five declared-row
  mismatches — application-site row unifies contaminating callee rows;
  the directional-edge law's class). A fully-annotated member keeps its
  quantified pre-registration: the signature price for polymorphic
  recursion. A group-exit sweep re-generalizes every member over the
  group's resolved cells. Census 0 at every generation; the bound-hit
  mover (parse_effect_list_from) is measured ROUNDS-resident —

  this discipline is its prerequisite. Micros 121/0; frontier 328/0;

- source: MENTL MARCH — the wheel judges its own generation in-process,
  and the dig that landed it healed three latent breaks (2026-07-26).
  The verb: `mentl march` walks the wheel's file set (fd_readdir's
  self/parent links skipped — the unfiltered walk descended src/./.
  forever, +2 bytes a level to the alloc ceiling), compiles it through
  the canonical converged stdin chain IN-PROCESS, STREAMS the emission
  through the new wat_to_file(fd) sink into .build/march/m2.wat, reads
  the census inside the same install, and verdicts against the last
  generation — its first CLEAN was against the bash march's own m2, so
  the in-process and exec invocation routes are proven byte-identical.
  wat_to_file is the third sink (write-through to an fd; fs_create_impl
  the streaming open): region-immune because bytes reach the host at
  each arm, which is the scope law now written at wat_to_string's decl
  — a whole-compile install of the COLLECTING sink banks segment
  pointers the emit phase's per-fn region resets zero, and that is
  exactly how the in-process battery (`mentl test <dir>`) had been
  SILENTLY BROKEN since the emit-arena landed (its own gate never
  re-ran; the board's micros ran through the bash loop). The battery
  streams per-micro wat through the same sink now: 114/114 in-process.
  The explicit-stack concat drain (flat_fill_concat — call depth =
  representation alternation, never concat depth; proven on a
  5000-deep rope) and the correctly-rounded float family (callee-first
  order + Float pins on parse_float/parse_float_body/parse_mantissa_f64
  — the DAG path's single-pass judgment floored the f64 results of
  loose forward schemes to words, an assembly refusal in mentl run)
  landed through the same arc. Named en route with its 9-line RED
  fixture: Hβ.lower.install-config-capture-read — an install-config
  arg that references a CLOSURE CAPTURE reads 0 silently (a let-local
  reads true; march_emit binds sink_fd locally until the init path
  rides the capture ladder).

- source: ZERO PROSE LOST — the weave conserves the whole wheel
  (2026-07-25; the comment arc's terminus: 2,803 → 0, verified by the

  nested if's attached prose itself, uncuddling the chain at exactly
  the prose-carrying link (the lexer's two-char table comments were

  weave pass's span-tie rule (latest mint wins) resolves a body-head
  comment to the LIVE match instead of the dissolved let — the last
  silent prose-drop class, and the callers' nexpr wraps delete with
  the contract change. The fmt summit's PROSE gate is fully open:

- source: THE WEAVE INVERTS — one attachment pass, one emission point
  (2026-07-25; Morgan's "isn't there a better way" answered by
  deleting the whole per-site family). The nine hand-closed comment
  families were one law fought site by site; the inversion replaces
  them: attach_comment_weave runs ONCE after parse_program (every
  route), resolving each comment run by SPAN against the node weave —
  trailing runs to the widest node starting on their line, leading
  runs to the widest node at the minimal start past the run, span
  ties to the latest mint (which also retires the desugar transfer:
  the replacing match out-mints the dead let), and a blank-separated
  earlier block INHERITS the next run's target (each node competes
  only for its nearest run). One O(N log C) scan of the span index
  serves every run. EMISSION went uniform too: render_tokens_for —
  the one entry every node's render flows through — prefixes the
  node's attached prose; the per-construct emission calls delete
  (rows keep theirs: op/variant cnodes never flow through the entry),
  and prose-forced breaks now CASCADE from fits_inline seeing the
  emitted newline instead of per-construct special cases. The verb
  writes its own FIXPOINT: fmt renders, re-parses, re-renders, and
  writes the second render, so a mid-line attach moves to its
  line-start home internally and one invocation is idempotent by
  construction. ~50 parse/render sites deleted (the collects, the
  probe-law contortions, attach_leading_prose/attach_trailing_comment/
  collect_leading_comments/same_line_back whole); the parser's skips
  are comment-transparent again. TWO artifact-taught corrections en
  route: the run record went NOMINAL (CwRun — the generic bsearch
  walkers compile once, so an open element floored their field reads;
  the brand + param Intent Boundaries prove the offsets), and the
  first form lost blank-separated heads until the inherit rule
  landed. MEASURED: conservation 1,969 → 741 → 106 lost lines across
  the whole wheel (vs the per-site machinery's 35 with ~50 more
  sites), IDEMPOTENT-ALL-50, and the whole board green.

- source: THE CHAIN BREAKS AT PROSE + THE GATE NAMES ITS LOSSES
  (2026-07-25; the operator-interior family and the report that
  retired the external diff for good). Prose after a binary operator
  belongs to the RIGHT operand: binop_loop — the ONE path pipes and
  binops share — collects the run after the operator and attaches it
  to the operand's node, and the renders break the chain there: a
  prose-carrying operand puts the operator at line end, the comment
  above the operand (the || ladder shape), and the chain renders
  (converge/tee) emit stage prose above the verb line at stage
  indent (render_stage_comment). The conservation gate now NAMES its
  losses: on a shortfall, fmt prints each source comment line whose
  text appears nowhere in the render's lex ("  lost: // …", up to
  eight) — the verb replaced the last hand diff, and its first
  report immediately identified the remaining ~70 lines as trailing
  drift-audit markers (load-bearing: the audit hook reads them
  per-line), argument-interior trailing series, and one block shape.

  chain renders' declared rows widened to their bodies' new weave

- source: DEPTH IS COMPOSITION + FITS-OR-BREAKS — the layout engine's
  two primitives land (2026-07-25; the width regression falls from
  2× to near-parity). REINDENT-ON-COMPOSE: every interior renders at
  depth ZERO and each enclosing block indents its whole interior
  exactly once at composition (indent_block) — depth is never
  threaded and never wrong, because nesting depth IS composition
  depth (the old fixed two-space literals rendered a nested match's
  arms at column 2 at any depth; the probe now shows depth-correct
  nesting to level four). The six wrappers recompose: block, match,
  effect decl, handler decl, vertical variants, prose-else.
  FITS-OR-BREAKS: an inline render survives when it is one line
  within the 100-column budget, else the construct takes its block
  layout — fn and let break after `=` with the body indented; an
  overflowing if takes the cuddled-block vertical (`} else if c {`,
  SYNTAX's own chase_node shape) through
  render_branch_block/render_else_block; an overflowing call breaks
  one argument per line; a long prose-free variant set takes the
  vertical layout. The budget measures at depth zero (a render never
  knows its depth under reindent-on-compose) — the honest
  approximation, stated at the fits_inline site. MEASURED: formatted
  non-comment lines over 100 columns fell 1,335 → 628 against an
  authored baseline of 568, with IDEMPOTENT-ALL-50 held at every
  rung and the march CLEAN through all three pins of the arc. The
  remaining swap-gate is the ~65 operator-interior prose lines
  (is_seq_op's || ladder and kin) — the chain-break-at-prose form.

- source: THE ARM'S ARROW STOPS EATING + THE GATE GOES LEXICAL
  (2026-07-25; the ninth comment family, and the conservation gate
  trued to the line). Prose between an arm's `=>` and its body — the
  block above a multi-line arm body, the single biggest remaining
  class (lower.mn alone held 116 lines) — collects at both arm loops'
  arrows and attaches to the same body handle the leading run uses;
  the weave merges, the arm render emits it above the arm, and the
  reparse re-attaches it there. lower.mn's loss went to ZERO and
  infer.mn's 84 → 31. THE GATE'S OWN BLIND SPOT, found by
  cross-checking its verdict against the independent line census: a
  weave-to-weave compare sees only what the parse ATTACHES — a line
  the parse still consumes as layout is invisible on BOTH sides,
  conserved-by-blindness (and block-counting also misread merges as
  drops). The metric went LEXICAL: the lexer emits one TComment per
  authored prose line, so fmt lexes source and render and compares
  token counts — the difference IS the lost lines, including
  never-attached ones; the weave-walk fns deleted with the count
  metric. The verb's verdict now matches the external census
  line-for-line ("31 of 2079 prose lines would not survive") and the
  python census retires for real. THE REMAINING ~65 LINES ARE ONE
  CLASS: prose interleaved in OPERATOR chains (is_seq_op's `||`

- source: THE VERB CARRIES ITS OWN CENSUS — fmt gains the prose
  conservation gate, and the else/file-tail families land

  the DAG's other modules never skew the count), counts the weave's
  attached blocks on both sides, and REPORTS the verdict itself:

- source: THE ATTACH TARGET IS THE LIST'S NODE — the mid-block
  statement family lands (2026-07-25; the sixth comment family, and

  expression statement wraps in a fresh ExprStmt node AFTER the leg's
  leading attach ran — the prose landed on the inner expr's handle
  while the block render reads the wrapper's, so every mid-block
  statement comment dropped while let/fn statements carried theirs
  (measured: "before the unit if" vanished in one pass; the fix
  attaches per-branch to the node the statement list will hold).
  IDEMPOTENT-ALL-50 holds; the line-count metric SATURATED (formatted
  now carries MORE comment lines than authored — trailing prose
  becoming its own line inflates lines while preserving content), so
  the honest measure moved to the content-matched census: 513
  authored comment lines whose text does not survive, bucketed —
  file-TAIL blocks (155; no following decl — the Module-handle
  attach + end-of-render emission is the fix), before else/else-if
  (150; the else probe's skip consumes the run — the else-branch
  node is the target), before match heads in expression positions
  (45), variants' inline-render residue (19), and comments inside
  multi-line call argument lists (~80 across the other buckets —
  the genuine expression-interior tail).
  Hβ.parser.expr-interior-comment-attach carries exactly those
  named shapes, each with the recipe this arc proved six times:
  collect at the position the old skip consumed, attach to the node
  the render reads, render where the parse re-collects.

- source: THE PROSE JOINS THE WEAVE — five comment families attach,
  ops and variants become addressable, and the probe law lands
  (2026-07-25; the summit's 2,803 dropped comment lines fall to 469 —
  83% recovered — with IDEMPOTENT-ALL-50 held throughout). The
  families, each RED-measured by the sweep's census before its fix:
  (1) MATCH ARMS (1,353 — the biggest class): an arm's leading prose
  attaches to the arm's BODY node (the arm's one handle; patterns are
  bare values), trailing same-line backward to the same handle; the
  caller's skip_ws was eating the run before the loop's collect ever
  ran. (2) HANDLER ARMS: the same recipe closes
  Hβ.parser.handler-arm-doc-attachment — a seed-era "for now" skip
  that named its own peer. (3) BRACELESS-CHAIN CONTINUATIONS (344):
  the expression-position let now runs the block loop's full
  discipline (trailing backward to the let node, leading forward to
  the continuation's first statement), and desugar_block TRANSFERS a
  dissolving destructure-let's prose to the arm body (the render puts
  it above the pattern that WAS the let — movement, never loss).
  (4+5) EFFECT OPS (677) and TYPE VARIANTS (312) become ADDRESSABLE:
  each row widens with its own minted NTypeAnn node (the op's return
  type / the variant's payload product — the row's genuine type fact,
  its handle in the weave); the census named all thirteen consumer
  destructures and the sweep converged in four compiles. THE
  NON-CONSUMING PROBE LAW, paid for by the alternating-drop the
  memory.mn idempotence diff measured (every arrowless op swallowed
  its successor's prose): a lookahead probe (the effect-op arrow, both
  arm loops' comma) returns the PRE-probe position on a miss — a scan
  is a read, never a consume. The vertical variant layout renders
  prose-carrying ADTs one variant per line (`=`/`|` leading, the
  connective owned by the variant render — the double-`=` was the
  first sweep's wreckage), and parse_type_decl routes the
  name-to-`=` run to the first variant's node. THE RATCHET'S OWN
  PROOF: the moment op/arm/variant prose entered the weave, the
  comment-refs gate JUDGED it — 16 phantoms fired on prose that had
  never been audited (backticked names in trailing op comments), each
  trued or unbackticked to 0. The named remainder (469): before
  if/else (286) and before match head (126) — expression-position
  runs needing render sites at the if/match projections — plus the
  before-closer and eof tails; Hβ.parser.expr-interior-comment-attach
  narrows to exactly that residue.

- source: IDEMPOTENT-ALL-50 — the fmt fixpoint reached, the summit's
  swap gated by its own comment law (2026-07-25; four render roots +
  one parse canonicalization, each convicted by the sweep). The whole
  wheel now formats to a byte-fixpoint in ONE pass. The roots, in
  landing order: (1) THE LET-CHAIN FLATTENS AT PARSE — a braceless
  let-chain parsed to nested per-let BlockExprs while the braced
  spelling parsed flat (two graphs for one meaning; the formatter
  faithfully rendered the redundancy as a brace pyramid); the
  continuation now merges into ONE statement sequence
  (parse_let_expr), Law-7 byte-identical — lower dissolves block
  nesting, so only the graph's spelling changed. (2) THE SURFACE-TYPE
  PROJECTION — the formatter borrowed show_type (the VOICE: List(T)
  spellings, tN@eN free-var handles, every row rendered), which leaked
  inferred internals into rendered source: an effect op's fn-typed
  param rendered `with t45323@e23311` and the second pass misparsed it
  into THREE bogus op declarations (silent semantic corruption through
  a formatter). render_type_tokens is the parse's inverse — [T] never
  List(T), fn-type params are TYPES (no invented `_:` names), a row
  renders only its AUTHORED present/absent content (a parse-minted
  tail var IS authored absence) — swapped in at all eight fmt type
  sites; PLAN §5.U's voice/format boundary note enforced at last.
  (3) A LAMBDA OPERAND ALWAYS RE-WRAPS (prec 0 in expr_prec_of + the
  render_chain_pos head guard at every chain renderer + render_operand
  on the generic pipe arm): the recurrence `((prev) => …) <~ delay(1)`
  rendered paren-free and pass 2 read `delay(1)(prev)` as an
  application. (4) A BODY-LEAD `{` IS THE BLOCK — a record literal or
  update as a fn/lambda/arm body re-wraps in the parens the parser
  consumed (render_grouped_body at the five body-lead surfaces);
  `= ({cmp: [], …})` had rendered bare and reparsed as a block of
  garbage holes. (5) INT_MIN renders its WRAPPING positive spelling
  (int_to_str's own minus re-parsed as unary minus and accreted one
  `-` per pass). THE ORACLES: idempotence ALL 50 wheel files
  (pass2 == pass1 byte-identical); the formatted tree compiles
  census-0 and its wat is value-identical to the pristine wheel's
  modulo three CLASSIFIED diffs — handle renumbering (names +
  provenance constants), the FNV -2128831035 const-fold firing in one
  tree and not the other (value-identical code shapes; an order-
  sensitive e-graph fold, banked with band G's typed-rulecyclic), and
  yield_from's open-record demand-order offsets (the named
  trecordopen class, internally consistent per compile). THE GATE
  THAT HOLDS THE SUMMIT: the formatted tree DROPS 2,803 comment lines
  (16.7% of authored prose — expression-interior comments consumed as
  layout, the named Hβ.parser.expr-interior-comment-attach) and
  doubles >100-char lines (572 → 1208; the width-aware layout
  engine); canonizing prose destruction is forbidden by the weave's
  own law, so the SWAP gates on those two builds — the gate doing its
  job, not deferral. The voicey carry leg re-banked to the
  SURFACE-canonical annotation spelling (the authored byte-identical
  `{kind: String, level: Int}`; the old expectation was show_type's
  voice spacing).

- source: THE TWO READERS AGREE — parse_float goes correctly rounded,
  the shortest render becomes the one float projection, and the
  list_set flat contract goes loud (2026-07-25; the fmt summit's
  idempotence sweep forced all three roots into the open). The summit's
  19 non-idempotent files reduced to ONE mechanism with THREE liars,
  each convicted by probe: (1) parse_float paid one rounding PER
  fractional digit — rebuilt as ONE f64-integer mantissa accumulation
  (exact through 2^53, every authored literal) + ONE scale by the exact
  power of ten (float_scale10, splitting past 10^22), proven EQUAL to
  IEEE division at the probe (parse("0.3") == 3.0/10.0); this closes
  Hβ.runtime.parse-float-correctly-rounded over the exact-mantissa
  range. (2) float_render_positive normalized by DIVIDING by
  float_pow10(exp<0) — an unrepresentable power (1/10 rounds) skewing
  scaled a whole ulp (0.3 normalized to 2.9999999999999996, not 3.0);
  a negative exponent now MULTIPLIES by the exact positive power, one
  correctly-rounded step, with a decade re-clamp. (3) THE ROOT UNDER
  THE ROOT: the shortest ascent's rounded-last bump list_set into a
  PUSH-BUILT digits list — and list_set computed FLAT addresses for
  every tag, so the bump wrote a digit over the snoc node's PARENT
  POINTER and never took effect (the ascent's round-last arm was dead
  since birth; measured: list_set(push(make_list(0),2),0,3)[0] == 2,
  while index 1 on a two-element snoc "worked" by layout coincidence —
  the payload slot sits where flat elem[1] would). The documented
  flat-only contract is now ENFORCED: list_set's base refuses a
  structured tag loudly (the census instrument for any other violator —
  the board ran clean, so the class had one instance); the extraction
  buffer is preallocated flat (make_list(nd), the buffer-counter
  idiom). With parse correct the shortest render's oracle
  (parse_float(cand) == f) gives the SAME verdict as the assembler on
  every short candidate — so float_to_str_bits is DELETED whole and
  the emit boundary reverts to float_to_str: the bits render existed
  only to compensate for the broken parse, and its own digit
  extraction was a ulp off in the tail anyway (true nearest-0.3
  rendered "...96", outside the rounding radius — the assembler landed
  one ulp LOW, which is why the wat-born 0.3 differed from the parsed
  one). Probe battery on the fixed wheel: parse("0.3")/lexed
  0.3/parse("0.995")/6.02/2.5/1.0/100.5/0.001 all render canonical
  short forms; 0.1+0.2 renders "0.30000000000000004"; READERS-AGREE.
  Named residue: the unverified nd=17 tail's float-stepped extraction
  (Hβ.runtime.float-render-17-digit-exact — never reached by a lexed
  literal, whose own spelling is a short witness). The march ruled
  TRANSITION (76 diff lines = the corrected f64 constants crossing one
  generation) and m3 == m4 held — the wheel's floats are now TRUE to
  their authored spellings for the first time.

  frontier 279/0; proof-exactness 9/9; crown 5/5;

- source: FMT RUNG 6 — THE SHORTEST FAITHFUL FLOAT + THE TWO-READER
  DISCOVERY (2026-07-25; the ladder's last rung, and the probe that
  found a real pre-existing divergence). float_to_str renders the
  FEWEST digits whose parse-back equals the value (1.0 → "1.0",
  3.14 → "3.14"; the ascent tries truncated then last-digit-rounded,
  seventeen digits terminate it) — the oracle is parse_float ITSELF,
  the lexer's own conversion, so fmt round-trips under the medium's
  real reader by construction. En route the probe convicted TWO
  pre-existing truths: digit_at's doc claimed a zero-pad its raw
  list_index never did (trued at the one reader — the first short
  digit list trapped the armed bounds check), and THE TWO READERS
  DISAGREE: parse_float("0.3") != the wat-born 0.3 (parse_float's
  naive digit/scale summation is not correctly rounded, while the
  assembler's conversion is) — named
  Hβ.runtime.parse-float-correctly-rounded. Until it closes, the
  EMIT boundary keeps the bits-faithful seventeen-digit projection
  (float_to_str_bits — an emitted constant is re-read by the
  assembler's correctly-rounded reader; a shortest form tuned to the
  naive reader could change the constant's value through emission),
  and values whose naive parse disagrees with their bits render
  longer-but-always-faithful at the human registers. The generic-show
  leg's banked "2.5000000000000000" was the inflation era's text —
  re-derived (2.5 is exact) and re-banked at "val: 2.5".

- source: HANDLER-CONFIG DEFAULTS — THE PRODUCT LAW AT THE HANDLER
  DECL (2026-07-25; the fleet's builder, cherry-picked three-way onto
  live main under the new base-freshness contract and re-derived).
  A handler's config list IS a parameter product, so its fields carry
  defaults: the carrier goes [String] → [TParam] — the fn-param
  carrier itself, ONE default representation (the TParam's fifth
  slot), ONE fill (resolve_call_args), zero second mechanisms.
  parse_config_params mirrors parse_one_param's scalar face
  (name [= default]; annotations/destructures stay the named
  lathe-lag, refused loudly as today); register_handler types each
  default in the handler's own scope under a DefaultReason edge (a
  bare ~> h has no call node to splice through); lower's
  extract_handler_config_args resolves the install product through
  the same seam infer's CallExpr path reads; the formatter renders
  name = default back. Four-face fixture: bare install fills (8),
  explicit fills (16), parens omitted fills all (6), labeled skips
  over (12) = 42. The stale-base tax showed once at re-derivation
  (render_config_params' row predated the intern era — one widened
  row) and the numbering TRANSITION crossed in the same march.

- source: THE UNSEEDED-OVERFLOW GUARD (2026-07-25; the bracket
  refutation's F1 hardened on the live arms). The planned-mint
  overflow jump is ROOT-ONLY semantics — only the root's plan seeds
  mint_high — so an overflow firing with mint_high still 0 is an
  unseeded instance (a future branch cursor past its range), and the
  jump would mint handle 0 over the shared Module root's cell. Both
  fresh arms now trap that case loudly (the deliberate cross-2^32
  load, the world-find-miss precedent) instead of minting; the branch
  protocol stays abort-and-requeue by design. Unreachable on every
  root path (the plan always seeds before limits exist) — the guard
  is the belt the refuter demanded before any branch instance is
  built.

  overlay state fields, the per-mint overlay_register_at write (two
  index reads + extend + three sets + a tuple allocation off the
  HOTTEST write path, ×~200k mints per self-compile), the graph_fork
  op and arm, the five overlay fns, the eager overlays_to_pairs build
  on every snapshot (twenty sites, nineteen of which ignored it), and
  the Graph snapshot's middle field — Graph(next, span_index). The
  checkpoint record shrinks 12 → 7 fields; the rollback restore
  10 → 5. The ONE real reader (QueueItem.module_path, filled at
  construction — a SNAPSHOT of a live fact) rewires to the live read:
  filter_by_module keys on module_path_of_span(parse_span_of(pos)) —
  NModule span containment, the fact the graph already carries;
  module_path_of_span moves to graph.mn (DAG-homed beside
  parse_span_of; oracle and cursor both import graph). Resolves

- source: THE LAYER-ORDERED FINAL (2026-07-25; Phase C rung 1 step
  C1c-1 — the fan's exact execution shape, run sequentially first).
  The trial's own reasons ARE the stmt DAG: every reference it
  resolved drew a VarLookup Reason, so a stmt's edges are the
  VarLookup names over its trial range — read from the graph through
  the new graph_reason_at (the unchased reason cell), never re-walked
  from the AST. Only BACKWARD edges order the walk (a forward fn
  reference resolves to the trial's published final scheme; the
  genuinely order-coupled stmts — top-level lets — precede their
  dependents in source), so depths compute in one ascending pass and
  the layer graph is acyclic by construction; a missing edge leaves
  equal depths whose within-layer source order preserves source
  behavior. The final pass now walks LAYER BY LAYER with
  source-order pre-assigned bases — the numbering stays
  plan-determined while the judgment order becomes the one the
  parallel fan will reproduce. The march arbitrated the reorder
  TRANSITION-grade: the layer-order judgment differs from the
  source-order judgment by 1,228 emit lines and reproduces ITSELF
  exactly (m3 == m4) — walk-order convergence proven by the
  fixpoint, which is precisely the gate C1c-2's concurrency will be
  held to.

- source: THE PLANNED MINT (2026-07-25; Phase C rung 1 step C1b — the
  deterministic handle partition goes live, sequential-planned). The
  trial IS the sizing oracle: each stmt's mint count is the graph_next
  delta around its trial inference (a fact the graph already carries,
  read at the boundary — zero instrumentation), and the final pass
  mints each stmt into a pre-assigned dense range [base, base+count),
  bases prefix-summed from the final's own post-prepass frontier. The
  numbering is a pure function of the source through the trial's
  determinism — the exact numbering the parallel fan (C1c) must
  reproduce, which is its byte-equality gate. Three graph ops carry
  the plan (mint_plan seeds the overflow target above every base;
  mint_at enters a range and raises the high-water past what it
  leaves; mint_seal resumes sequential at the high-water); a mint at
  a range's ceiling jumps to the open space and clears the limit
  (the decl finishes deterministically — decl order is); a stmt
  minting under its measure leaves virgin gap cells, absorbed by the
  word-face guard now on graph_node_at (the C1a Cast read). Handle
  space stays DENSE — no sparse encoding, no iterator migration, no
  storage change: the partition is a NUMBERING plan over the C1a
  pages, fully decoupled from physical paging. Self-compile 5.57s
  median / 515MB — flat.

  the resuming process's dead pre-swap heap must read as never

- source: THE FANOUT SPAWNS FOR REAL (2026-07-24; the task-record
  landing — `>< ~> parallel_compose` runs branches on host threads over
  ONE shared image, and the whole phantom glue dies). The task record

  through the ONE allocator: $spawn_task_impl builds it, hands its
  address to the thread-spawn import, refuses loudly on a host spawn
  failure; the rewritten $wasi_thread_start stamps the per-instance
  identity ($tid_g), runs $__init_lets when lets exist, invokes the task
  through the closure protocol, stores the result, releases the
  completion word; $join_task_impl atomic-waits and reads it. The
  module's MEMORY OWNERSHIP follows its own proof (spawn_task in the
  fifth projection): a spawning module IMPORTS the shared image
  (re-exported for the p1 ABI) and allocates through the shared CELL at
  address 64 (compare-exchange bump — per-instance globals cannot serve
  two instances of one image); a thread-free module keeps its defined
  memory, plain bump, and ships NO thread-spawn import at all.
  heap_mark/heap_reset go strategy-invariant at the call site
  ($heap_mark_impl beside $alloc). threading.mn rewrote whole: the
  ffi/sentinel/intrinsic block, the write-only threads ledger, num_cores
  (no truth source under preview1), and the bypassed wasi_threads
  handler are DELETED; WaitResult's decl order now matches the wait32

- source: THE FORMATTER SPEAKS — render totality + `mentl fmt`
  (2026-07-23; Hβ.format.render-totality-before-fmt's first landing).
  The 18 missing render arms land (Expr 25/25, Stmt 9/9, Pat 8/8 — the
  three surrender-fallbacks DELETED into exhaustive enumeration), and
  first light on the dormant module surfaced four latent bugs, each
  fixed: every list joiner rendered REVERSED (last/drop_last walks over
  source-ordered lists — zero callers had ever seen it); `{{` is not an
  escape (the file's own idiom emitted doubled braces; SYNTAX's \{ is
  the escape — 22 sites swept, the pre-existing IfExpr arm included);
  string content never re-escaped (format∘parse = identity was false
  for any string carrying a quote or newline); and
  `graph_node_at(handle)` typechecked against an op binding while the
  direct-call tier emitted the two-arg raw fn — the op/fn name
  collision class (Hβ.emit.op-fn-name-collision-direct-call, named),
  surfaced the moment format_default gained its first installer.
  PRECEDENCE-INVERSE parens: an operand looser than its parent (or
  equal on the right of left-assoc) re-wraps in the parens the parse
  consumed — (a+b)*c and a+b*c both typecheck, so the gate is
  BEHAVIORAL: the fixture compiles and runs to 42 before AND after the
  render. The render carries the comment weave (decl + interior; a
  trailing comment re-renders leading — movement not loss, stable from
  pass two) and authored param annotations (fields re-sorted to the
  parse's own alphabetical canon). assemble_render gained String
  intent boundaries (the fan's tuple elements reach it free — the §9
  pointer-eq law's sibling). Named refinements: sugar preservation
  (a destructuring let re-renders as its desugared match — the graph's
  truth; byte-fidelity needs the sugar recorded), the width-aware
  layout engine, and the summit — the wheel formatting ITSELF into
  canonical source. En route the fixture walked into the standing
  trecordopen-wrong-field class in its lambda shape (a lambda param's
  field read on a plain record returns garbage — the peer's repro set
  grows one shape; annotated-closed dodges it).

- source: THE COMMENT WEAVE AT THREE ALTITUDES (2026-07-23). SYNTAX's
  "never dropped" is now true for the measured gap: a block-INTERIOR
  comment attaches to the finest FOLLOWING node (the next statement,
  the final expr, or the block's own unit node — attachment by HANDLE,
  so the comment-before-final-expr case never wraps the final as an
  ExprStmt), and a TRAILING same-line comment attaches BACKWARD to the
  node whose line it shares (same-line decided by token geometry — no
  TNewline between item and comment; span comparison failed because a
  braceless let-chain leaves the position past consumed newlines, the
  str_contains doc attaching to str_compare until the token test).
  The address surface renders the attached prose's first line as the
  Lede facet (the one comment_first_line projection — a duplicate lede
  helper died to E_DuplicateFnName's own refusal). Interior comments
  thereby joined the backtick gate, and the referent set grew its
  SCOPE face: each top-level decl contributes (extent, comment_locals)
  — extents from consecutive stmt starts, since decl spans are
  head-only — so an interior comment's backticks resolve against the
  enclosing fn's params and binders; the eight residual phantoms were
  the narration class (other-scope and historical names) and
  unbackticked per the contract. The affine ledger's
  destructure-arm/sibling-move pairing forced the block loop
  destructure-free (helpers own the collects — the pattern is banked
  in the loop's own comment).

  Pure — every effectful stored closure then "violated Pure." The fix

  (parse_type_ty had minted it as a TYPE handle, which free_in_row never
  collects and instantiate cannot freshen) and quantifies it into the ctor
  scheme; result_ty filters row handles out by node-kind so the type stays
  non-parametric, and instantiate's is_row_handle -> mint_row freshens the row
  per use, so a table of heterogeneous-row closures unifies by the row algebra.
  This UNBLOCKED the mentl CLI overhaul: the verb set had three drifting homes
  (the hand-written verb_catalog help, the `if mode == "..."` parse chain, the

  ParseError that names the missing argument) and the help catalog, so they
  cannot drift. Witnessed RED->GREEN on the wheel's own census: the verb table

  the native body at full speed, spill the wide result), so a polymorphic

Prior pin's provenance:

- source: OWNERSHIP STOPS FIGHTING SAFE CODE (2026-07-17, the affine model,
  §11 col 2). 115 of the 152 `E_OwnershipViolation` "consumed twice" errors
  were false positives — the medium was stricter than Rust on provably-safe
  code, the opposite of §4⑤'s Hylo-quiet thesis. Two masked bugs: (1) `if` /
  `match` arms were never bracketed as branches, so an `own` value read in
  both arms of `if i<0 {slice(buf)} else {list_set(buf)}` counted as two
  consumes — but the arms are ALTERNATIVES (exactly one runs). A `BranchMode`
  ADT (BParallel | BAlternative) now rides the branch frame; branch_exit
  reports a cross-arm collision only for BParallel (`><` / `<|`, all arms run),
  unions without collision for BAlternative. (2) affine_ledger is installed
  ONCE at the pipeline, not per body, so a name consumed in one function stayed
  in `used` and collided in the next naming the same `own` param — `buf` / `acc`
  are everywhere, the dominant shape. consume_enter_fn / consume_exit_fn now
  bracket each infer_fn body (a fresh consume scope, restored on exit,
  re-entrant for nested fn decls). Carried-Truth: the graph already knows a
  branch is an alternative and a body is a scope; the ledger re-derived neither.
  Census 727 -> 615; the `><` / `<|` collision path is unchanged (crown + the
  micro battery green). Emit grew (the ADT + two ops), a clean m2 == m3
  TRANSITION. RED-first gate: tests/frontier/mn-own-alternative-branches.mn
  (2 false "consumed twice" on the old boot, clean run to 30 on this one).
  Residue named (37 left): the move-vs-borrow-by-callee case — an `own` value
  read in an `if` condition or a field is a BORROW, not a move. THE BOARD IS WHOLE.

Prior pin's provenance:

- source: THE ALIAS EDGE IS READ, NOT RE-DERIVED (2026-07-17,
  `Hβ.infer.alias-preserving-unify`, §11 col 2). 362 census errors —
  `Span vs ValidSpan` / `Int vs ValidOffset`, both directions — were ONE
  root, the census's single biggest. `pre_register_decls` ran a single
  source-order pass, so a fn signature quantified before its refined alias
  was declared (parser.mn's `span: ValidSpan` precedes types.mn, which sorts
  last in the concatenated wheel) called `quantify_ctor_ty` against an env
  that did not yet hold the alias edge — and baked a bare nominal
  `TName("ValidSpan")` into both the param handle and the fn scheme. The main
  walk then tried to refine that bare name to its live
  `TAlias(ValidSpan, TRefined(Span, _))` and `unify(bare-TName, Span)` floored
  at the leaf. Fix: register the alias edges in a phase BEFORE any fn
  signature (`pre_register_alias`), so the resolver reads the LIVE edge.
  Carried-Truth exactly — the graph drew the alias; the resolver re-derived a
  name. The banked "a two-pass is a no-op" (2026-07-17, one session prior) was
  measuring BOOT's census (old inference) rather than the fixed compiler's;
  the fixed compiler reports 0 for the class, 1233 -> 874 total. Emit is
  byte-identical (an error hole and a resolved TAlias both lower at word
  width, same codegen path), so m2 == m3 held as a clean fixed point and boot
  was re-pinned only because the compiler binary changed (wheel-neutral yet
  tool-changing). Guarded RED-first:
  tests/frontier/mn-refined-alias-forward-ref.mn (a refined-alias param used
  before its type is declared) fails `Pos vs Int` on the old boot and runs to
  42 on this one. THE BOARD IS WHOLE.

Prior pin's provenance:

- source: THE SURFACE PARSES ITS OWN CANONICAL FORM (2026-07-17,
  `Hβ.parser.refined-alias-nonatomic-base`, §11 col 2). SYNTAX §«Type
  aliases»' own example `type NonEmpty = [a] where len(self) > 0` did not
  parse: parse_type_decl probed only `p2 + 1` for `where`, so a multi-token
  base (`[Int]` is three tokens) put `where` out of reach — P_UnexpectedToken
  on `where` at the `]`, then E_MissingVariable on the orphaned len/self. The
  fix parses the whole base type FIRST, then branches on what follows
  (`where` / `|`+`-`& / a ctor payload / nothing) — a `[base]` / `Option(k)`
  base now refines. The wheel uses no such form, so m2 == m3 stayed
  byte-identical and the march read a clean fixpoint; the parser fn's own
  bytes changed, though, so boot was stale until this re-pin (the case where
  a change is wheel-neutral yet tool-changing). Tooling improved WITH the
  medium in the same change: tests/frontier/mn-refined-alias-nonatomic.mn +
  its run_program registration guard the form (frontier 47 → 50). Verified in
  a pty: old boot failed the form, this boot exits 0. THE BOARD IS WHOLE.

Prior pin's provenance:

- source: MENTL RUNS FROM ANYWHERE — the install story is a pointer, the
  imports are the manifest (2026-07-16, ninth pin of the day; §11 column 1
  executed). Four landings in one arc: (1) the resolver chain
  (driver_module_path): direct existing-path → src/ → lib/ →
  /mentl-home/{src,lib} — the well-known guest path the install shim maps
  the repo to (an address, not an env var); (2) fs_at — the preopen table
  IS the mount table: absolute paths resolve by LONGEST guest-name prefix
  (the wasi-libc rule; fd_prestat_dir_name joins the import set), so the
  mapping is reachable — the old fs_dir_fd opened everything against the
  FIRST preopen; (3) prelude.mn declares its runtime imports (memory /
  strings / lists / threading) — the imports ARE the manifest, so a user
  program's one `import prelude` pulls the whole runtime through the dep
  walk; (4) driver_compile_entry — the compile verb reads the entry as the
  one-namespace CONCATENATION of its dep DAG (deps first, post-order — the
  wheel's own proven build form), so every dep's decls lower and emit
  (driver_check_entry lowered only the entry: $len undefined, the gap the
  first temp-dir run exposed). tools/install.sh writes ~/.local/bin/mentl,
  a POINTER to this boot — every re-pin is instantly the global CLI (the
  pin IS the release). MEASURED from a fresh temp dir: `mentl check` /
  `mentl compile` / `mentl < file` work; `mentl run t.mn` (import prelude,
  len([1,2,3])+39) → exit 42 through the shim's compile→assemble→execute
  half (WASI has no exec — the wheel's run verb names that seam);
  `mentl run hole.mn` REFUSES (exit 1, zero bytes, E_UnresolvedHole) —
  the refusal law survives the whole pipeline. Method notes re-paid: the
  stale-m3.wasm cp trap (assemble from the wat, NEVER cp on a clean fixed
  point) and one eprint-Heisenberg null. Shadow fingerprint re-pinned
  (6273b71e… — prelude's new imports shift the shadow); IDE shims gain the
  fd_prestat_dir_name stub. THE BOARD IS WHOLE at the bless.
